Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any immunology module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 601. The projective resolution has length at most 212 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 601 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the immunology structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 212. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
